<footer class="svelte-ek10gh" data-optly-47d87c59-1914-4e2f-b1aa-f6c0447ea323="" data-optly-f6c3a2af-00e9-49a8-9bf5-2ab47303d5a6=""><header class="section-header svelte-ng5ji9" data-optly-47d87c59-1914-4e2f-b1aa-f6c0447ea323="">
<h2 class="section-title svelte-ng5ji9" data-optly-47d87c59-1914-4e2f-b1aa-f6c0447ea323="">About Startups.co.uk</h2>
</header>
<p data-optly-47d87c59-1914-4e2f-b1aa-f6c0447ea323="" style="margin-top:2em; font-size:1.2em;">Startups is the UK's leading resource for starting a business. Covering everything from business ideas to exiting out, we can help you.
</p>
<p data-optly-47d87c59-1914-4e2f-b1aa-f6c0447ea323="" style="margin-top:2em; font-size:1.2em;">
Founded in 2000, for almost two decades we have provided information, inspiration and opportunity to new and serial entreprenuers.</p>
<p data-optly-47d87c59-1914-4e2f-b1aa-f6c0447ea323="" style="margin-top:2em; font-size:1.2em;">
So whether you're starting from scratch, unsure of how to set up your payroll, or just interested in finding out more about the UK startup scene, you're in the right place.</p></footer>
